          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Even after you place your rocket, you will not be able to fly just yet. To fly, you will need to fill the rocket with fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Oil is found in large amounts spouting over the ocean surface. Collect as much oil as you can, as oil is non-renewable, and you need a lot of it for each rocket launch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "After collecting enough oil (at least six buckets for a launch and return), you will need to refine the oil into fuel. To do this, you will need to craft a fuel refinery. Power it, then place the oil in the left slot, and it will be refined into fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel Refinery",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Shift-click on the rocket to open its inventory, then place three buckets of fuel into the input slot. Place the other three buckets into the rocket's inventory for a return trip.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Making Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Etrionic Blast Furnace",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "The Etrionic Blast Furnace is an upgraded version of the blast furnace. It has two modes: blasting and alloying. Blasting works like a blast furnace, but with 4 slots for 4x efficiency. Alloying combines materials to make an alloy.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Steel is made in an etrionic blast furnace by combining iron and coal. This will make 1 steel ingot per iron and coal. Steel is essential for most recipes and is the main material of your first rocket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "At this point, you can launch your rocket; however, you will likely die very fast, as there is no oxygen on the moon. In order to survive on the moon, you will need an oxygenated space suit and an ample oxygen supply.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "To obtain oxygen, it must be extracted from water. An oxygen loader is required to do this. Craft one, then place water in the left tank, and watch it convert into oxygen. You must repeat this process many times until you have enough oxygen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Loader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "Oxygen can then be extracted by collecting it in a bucket, spacesuit, or a gas tank. After you have enough oxygen, you are moments away from launching your first rocket!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources.json",
          "text": "Portable Resources",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Portable Resources",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources.json",
          "text": "Gas Tanks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Portable Resources",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources.json",
          "text": "Oxygen tanks allow you to store oxygen in a convenient portable form. Despite the name, they can store any fluid. Holding down right-click will distribute the oxygen into items in your inventory, with a bias towards armor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Portable Resources",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources.json",
          "text": "Etrionic Capacitor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Portable Resources",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources.json",
          "text": "The Etrionic Capacitor is a battery-like item for storing energy in a portable form. It can be placed in the energy slot of machines to power them, and like the gas tanks, can be distributed into your inventory by holding down right-click. It features two modes: Sequential and Round Robin. Sequential will distribute energy one by one while Round Robin will distribute energy evenly. The mode can be changed by right-clicking while sneaking.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Portable Resources",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch.json",
          "text": "Preparing to Launch",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Preparing to Launch",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch.json",
          "text": "Follow these steps very carefully! Missing even one thing here could make your life tough, or worse, cause you to be stranded on the moon! In order to return to earth from the moon, you need two basic things: a launch pad and three buckets of fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Preparing to Launch",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch.json",
          "text": "Place these items in the inventory of your rocket, and ensure that you always have them every time you launch a rocket. Finally, you should bring multiple full gas tanks, plentiful food, soul torches or glowstone (normal torches will burn out), weapons and tools. Once you have this, you are ready to launch! Enter the rocket and press !",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Preparing to Launch",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/radio",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/radio.json",
          "text": "Before launching, why not listen to some tunes? The radio is a block that'll play real internet-streamed radio stations! There are a few stations that you can listen to, in multiple genres. In addition, anyone in the vicinity will hear the same station. Eventually, you'll be able to use the radio to craft the rover, which features a built-in radio for driving around and listening to music.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Radio",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The Moon",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The Moon",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "Congratulations on reaching the moon! The moon is a barren wasteland, grey and dead, as far as the eye can see; however, underground is where you will find the next major resource in Ad Astra - Desh! Desh is used to craft advanced machines, space stations, and the tier two rocket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The environment",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The environment of the moon is very different from earth. As the moon has no oxygen and is freezing, attempting to place water will freeze it and lit blocks like torches will break. Any plant life and crops will also die very quickly. To combat these effects, you can build an oxygen distributor, which will we cover later.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "Returning Home",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "To return back to earth, open your lander (shift + right-click) and collect the rocket, fuel and the launch pad. To remove the lander, punch it. Set up the launch pad, place the rocket down and fill it up with fuel. Press and fly back to earth.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket.json",
          "text": "Your First Rocket",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Your First Rocket",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket.json",
          "text": "NASA Workbench",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Your First Rocket",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket.json",
          "text": "In order to build a rocket, you must first collect all of the parts: cone, plates, fins, tanks and engine. You can then make the rocket in a NASA workbench, a specially designed workbench just for rockets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Your First Rocket",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket.json",
          "text": "NASA Workbench",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Your First Rocket",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket.json",
          "text": "To place your newly-created rocket, you must build a launchpad.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Your First Rocket",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/your_first_rocket.json",
          "text": "After the launch pad has been built, place the rocket in the center. If you want, you can power any part of the launch pad to automatically launch the rocket once fueled.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Your First Rocket",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flags",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Like cables, fluid pipes also have a duct block for transfering fluids safely in and out of sealed structures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {

          "text": "The gravity normalizer allows you to create artificial gravity in a sealed area, similar to the oxygen distributor.           "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables.json",
          "text": "Cables allow for long-range energy transfer. There are two variants: steel and desh – desh, having a higher transfer rate. When used with a wrench, cables can be configured to extend in directions or to disconnect from nodes in the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cables",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables.json",
          "text": "Cable ducts",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cables",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables.json",
          "text": "Cables are not solid blocks. As such, they will not seal a structure which prevents oxygen distributors and gravity normalizers from functioning. The cable duct is a full block that works exactly like cables while preventing leaks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cables",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Throughout the moon, There are large, inhabited underground villages. These villages contain an alien form of the earth villager, who call themselves Lunarians. Unlike their earth counterpart, Lunarian don't need oxygen and can survive the chilling environment of the moon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian have different trades than earth villagers, featuring planet-specific materials, space suits and oxygen gear.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Wandering Trader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian Wandering Traders appear on every moon and planet. They have many rocket-related trades that may save your life, including launch pads, oxygen, fuel, soul torches and water. They accept emeralds, which are commonly found in the various structures on the moon and planets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",

          "count": 5

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/mars.json",
          "text": "Now that you have explored the moon and worked with desh, you want to take it further and go to the red planet. Building a tier two rocket capable of travelling to Mars is similar to building the tier one rocket, but with desh materials instead of steel. Construct the parts, build the rocket in the NASA workbench, fuel it up, and off you go to Mars!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Mars",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },

          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/mars.json",
          "text": "Ostrum is the next material that you seek on the red planet. It is used to craft more powerful space armour, build exceptional machines and to construct a rocket capable of withstanding extreme heat, allowing you to travel to hot planets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Mars",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2

          "text": "The oxygen distributor is a powerful machine that distributes oxygen in a sealed room, allowing any life inside to survive. It works exactly like an oxygen loader, where you insert water into the tank, but instead of giving you oxygen, the oxygen is distributed into the air.",

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Water Pump",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "The water pump is essential for keeping the oxygen distributor running infinitely. To use it, place an infinite water source below, then place the water pump above one of the water blocks, and then place the oxygen distributor adjacent to it. Make sure to supply both the pump and the oxygen distributor with sufficient power.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Distributor and Pump",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Using the Distributor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Once you have supplied the oxygen distributor with an infinite water source, sufficient energy and a completely sealed room, it will distribute oxygen in that room. To check that oxygen is being distributed, click \"show\" in the distributor GUI. If the room is highlighted, that means that the oxygen has been successfully distributed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "If there is a hole in your structure that is causing oxygen to escape, the distributor GUI will provide a warning. Patch the hole to avoid unnecessary power draw and oxygen usage. After that, you should be able to take off your oxygen suit and survive. Each oxygen distributor can support up to 6,000 blocks, allowing you to build a moderately sized space station or base. If you need more, you can place more oxygen distributors.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Inside your oxygenated structure, you should provide proper ventilation to ensure that oxygen does not get blocked when a door is closed. To do this, you can create a one-block gap in between walls or use a vent block to allow oxygen flow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Rapid depressurization",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "When your structure is fully sealed, and a hole is created, your structure will rapidly depressurize. This can be a catastrophic event. All entities inside will be violently sucked out of the hole and die without a space suit. The oxygen distributor will also rapidly drain oxygen and power. Avoid this by patching holes as soon as possible and by preventing accidental holes from being created.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover.json",
          "text": "The rover is a land vehicle capable of driving on the rough terrain of moons and planets. It's exceptionally fast, has an inventory capable of storing 16 items and has two seats for multiple riders. You can also run over mobs and deal some serious damage. To use it, fill the rover with fuel (shift-click and place fuel buckets in the left input slot).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Rover",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover.json",
          "text": "Rover Recipe",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Rover",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover.json",
          "text": "The rover has a built-in radio for playing real internet streamed radio stations. Use it by interacting with the radio on the rover or by pressing (). If multiple players are in the rover, they'll hear the same radio as well.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Rover",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel.json",
          "text": "Solar Panel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Solar Panel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel.json",
          "text": "Solar Panel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Solar Panel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel.json",
          "text": "The solar panel is an upgraded version of the coal generator, producing more energy per tick while being self-reliant. Its power generation depends on the planet's distance from the sun and fog. Because of this, it is more efficient to place solar panels close to the sun. Solar panels also produce more energy in orbit, as there is no atmosphere blocking the light.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Solar Panel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel.json",
          "text": "Solar Panel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Solar Panel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/solar_panel.json",
          "text": "Check the GUI to see how much energy is being produced in the current world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Solar Panel",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "Space Station",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "Space Station",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "A space station is a pre-built structure that is created in the planet screen, provided you have the necessary materials. Space stations are always located in a moon or planet's orbit and provide the ability to place blocks in that orbit dimension.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "Space Station",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "Space stations are designed to be expanded upon, allowing you to build the base of your dreams over a moon/planet. All of your space stations are present in the planet screen. Other players are not able to spawn new space stations in the same area as yours, so your structure is safe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "Moving in Space",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "Physics in space are very different from earth. In this zero-gravity environment, you'll float around and not be affected by gravity or friction. This can make it difficult to move around. One tool that can help you is the zip gun. This takes oxygen and will rapidly consume it to propel you forward. This allows you to move around in space. Careful to control your speed. As there's no friction, you can go flying out and get lost.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "Zip Gun",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "If Cadmus is installed, the 3x3 chunks in your space station will be claimed. In addition, players will not be able to create new space stations in any of your claimed chunks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/space_station.json",
          "text": "If Argonauts is installed is installed, other players in your party or guild will be able to land at your space station, and you'll be able to land at theirs.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Space Station",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/ti_69",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/ti_69.json",
          "text": "Ti-69",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ti-69",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/ti_69",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/ti_69.json",
          "text": "Ti-69",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Ti-69",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/ti_69",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/ti_69.json",
          "text": "The Ti-69 is a mobile device that'll display the local oxygen, temperature, and gravity. Use it to check if the area is safe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ti-69",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/ti_69",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/ti_69.json",
          "text": "Ti-69 Recipe",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Ti-69",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal.           "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy.           "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",

          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",

          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },

          "text": "Sliding doors are large 3x3 doors. They seal oxygen when closed, making them great candidates for airlocks. They can be locked by shift-right-clicking them with a wrench. Locked doors can only be opened with redstone.",

          "text": "Globes are trophy blocks found as a reward at the end of a dungeon. Globes offer no practical function and are instead a decoration block.           "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury.json",
          "text": "Once you have enough Ostrum to craft a complete netherite space suit set and all of the components of the tier three rocket, you can travel to Venus and Mercury. Please note that if you enter these planets without a netherite space suit or fire resistance, you will die very fast. The next material that you seek is Calorite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Venus and Mercury",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury.json",
          "text": "Calorite is used to craft the ultimate rocket capable of travelling to a new solar system.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Venus and Mercury",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates.json",
          "text": "Creating Plates",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates.json",
          "text": "Once you've got energy, you'll want to craft a compressor. This machine can compress ingots and blocks into plates, which are needed to craft many things.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Even after you place your rocket, you will not be able to fly just yet. To fly, you will need to fill the rocket with fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Oil is found in large amounts spouting over the ocean surface. Collect as much oil as you can, as oil is non-renewable, and you need a lot of it for each rocket launch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "After collecting enough oil (at least six buckets for a launch and return), you will need to refine the oil into fuel. To do this, you will need to craft a fuel refinery. Power it, then place the oil in the left slot, and it will be refined into fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel Refinery",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Shift-click on the rocket to open its inventory, then place three buckets of fuel into the input slot. Place the other three buckets into the rocket's inventory for a return trip.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Generating Energy",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Generating Energy",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Coal Generator",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Generating Energy",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Energy is core resource of Ad Astra. It's used in all machines and is required for everything. There are multiple ways to generate energy, the most basic way being the coal generator. It uses burnable resources like wood or coal to generate energy.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Generating Energy",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Making Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Etrionic Blast Furnace",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "The Etrionic Blast Furnace is an upgraded version of the blast furnace. It has two modes: blasting and alloying. Blasting works like a blast furnace, but with 4 slots for 4x efficiency. Alloying combines materials to make an alloy.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Steel is made in an etrionic blast furnace by combining iron and coal. This will make 1 steel ingot per iron and coal. Steel is essential for most recipes and is the main material of your first rocket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "At this point, you can launch your rocket; however, you will likely die very fast, as there is no oxygen on the moon. In order to survive on the moon, you will need an oxygenated space suit and an ample oxygen supply.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "To obtain oxygen, it must be extracted from water. An oxygen loader is required to do this. Craft one, then place water in the left tank, and watch it convert into oxygen. You must repeat this process many times until you have enough oxygen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Loader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "Oxygen can then be extracted by collecting it in a bucket, spacesuit, or a gas tank. After you have enough oxygen, you are moments away from launching your first rocket!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/portable_resources.json",

        {
